1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the elements space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 400 and the convergence rate is O(825/n^3).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on elements structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 400. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 825. QED.
